## v0.7.182 — In-app Lower Third REMOVED + Auto-route detected refs to Chapter Navigator + Live Display red ring removed + Verse-text auto-fit on overflow

Four bundled operator requests landed in one ship after explicit per-change approval per the v0.7.180 GR-D process (diagnose → propose with proof → wait for "Yes ship" → ship).

**1. In-app Lower Third REMOVED — operator: "remove the lower third from the app, leaving only the lower third that can be operated in NDI settings only. Anywhere there's a lower third on the app, remove it and let users use it in only NDI settings."**

Three in-app surfaces stripped, NDI Output panel intentionally untouched:

- **Type narrowing — `src/lib/store.ts:46`**: `DisplayMode = 'full' | 'lower-third' | 'lower-third-black'` → `DisplayMode = 'full'`. The TypeScript compiler then surfaces every dead in-app LT branch as a type error so each call site is handled by deletion, not by re-widening the type. The separate `ndiDisplayMode: 'full' | 'lower-third'` field on the store stays as-is — NDI broadcast keeps its own LT mode driven by the NDI Output panel.
- **Persist migration v4 → v5 — `src/lib/store.ts:1188 + 1272-1290`**: any persisted `settings.displayMode` of `'lower-third'` or `'lower-third-black'` (carried over from older builds) silently coerces to `'full'` on first boot of v0.7.182. Idempotent: a fresh install at version 5 already has `displayMode='full'` and skips the coercion. NDI's separate `ndiDisplayMode` is intentionally NOT touched.
- **Display Mode picker DELETED — `src/components/views/settings.tsx` ~line 786-805 (~25 lines removed, comment block left in source)**: previously a 3-button picker (Full Screen / Lower Third / Lower Third (Black)). With LT now NDI-only, the picker collapses to a single 'Full Screen' option, so the entire control was removed.
- **Top-bar Output Display Mode dropdown DELETED — `src/components/layout/easyworship-shell.tsx` lines 966-1030 (~65 lines removed) + `modeMenuOpen` state cleanup at line 327-329**: previously a Popover with Full Screen / Lower Third / Lower Third · Black options. The status pill at line 1477 (`{settings.displayMode === 'full' ? 'Full Screen' : 'Lower Third'}`) collapsed to literal `Full Screen`. Header now goes Output Display button → NDI button directly with no LT picker between.
- **Lower Third Settings card DELETED — `src/components/views/settings.tsx` lines 1008-1358 (~320 lines removed)**: entire `<Card>` containing Position picker (Bottom/Top), Height picker (Small/Medium/Large), Typography card (Font, Size, Drop Shadow, Align), Bible Color picker, Bible Line-Height slider, Bible Text Scale slider, and the LIVE PREVIEW box. Persisted `lowerThird*` keys remain on disk (no destructive migration), but `USE_LT_OVERRIDES = !IS_NDI && dm.indexOf('lower-third')===0` is now permanently false in-app because dm = 'full' always — the values are inert until/unless a future build re-surfaces an in-app LT layer.
- **NDI Output panel — UNTOUCHED**: `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` not modified. `ndiDisplayMode` picker (Full / Lower Third) stays. `lowerThirdPosition / Height / Typography` controls stay (they live in the NDI panel UI now). `ndiLowerThird*` font/color/scale/transparent stay. OBS Browser Source URL bake from v0.7.181 stays. Renderer route.ts lower-third branch retained — driven exclusively by `IS_NDI && st.ndiDisplayMode === 'lower-third'`.

**2. Auto-route detected references to Chapter Navigator — operator: "Immediately a bible or bible reference is quoted are detected; also send it to Chapter Navigator."**

Single source-of-truth fix at `src/lib/store.ts:803-861` (`addDetectedVerse`): after the v0.7.119 dedupe `set()` call, fire `get().requestNavigatorRef(v.reference)` unconditionally when a reference exists. The bridge mechanism is unchanged — `requestNavigatorRef` writes `${ref}\u0000${Date.now()}` to `navigatorRequestedRef`, and the `BibleLookupCompact` `useEffect` at `library-compact.tsx:189-203` strips the timestamp suffix, parses the reference, calls `setSearchQuery(ref)` + `loadChapter(parsed.book, parsed.chapter, parsed.verseStart)`. Timestamp suffix guarantees React diffs the string even when the same ref re-detects, so the navigator re-jumps each time.

Five existing detection call sites in `speech-provider.tsx` (lines 1600 explicit refs, 1701 semantic owns-live, 1836 sticky live, 2014 follow-mode, 2069 promote-from-candidate) all get the auto-nav for free without per-site edits. Any future caller of `addDetectedVerse` also gets it for free. Atomicity safe: `get().requestNavigatorRef` is defined later in the same store factory; runtime closure resolution guarantees it exists by the time any detection fires.

`addDetectedVerseCandidate` is intentionally NOT auto-nav'd — those are <50% confidence guesses and would thrash the navigator on every speech tick. Manual click on a Detected Verses row already calls `requestNavigatorRef` (logos-shell.tsx L1935/1938/1949) so the bridge is proven; v0.7.182 just adds the auto-fire upstream.

**3. Live Display red ring removed — operator's earlier postimg flagged the inset red ring on the Live Display surface as visual noise that doesn't help operators**

`src/components/presenter/stable-stage.tsx:160` — removed `isLive && 'ring-2 ring-red-500 ring-inset'` from the className composition. The `isLive` prop is kept as a no-op for caller-compat (caller still passes it; component just ignores it cosmetically). Sister red-ring sites at `slide-renderer.tsx:452/464` and `library-compact.tsx:710` are intentionally LEFT ALONE — those are different surfaces (preview thumbnails / library item highlights) where the ring is operator-useful. The removal is Live Display-specific per the operator's explicit scope.

**4. Verse-text auto-fit on overflow — `src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` (~95 lines added inside the inline output script)**

`fitText()` + `autoFitTextEverywhere()` helpers measure `scrollHeight` vs container `clientHeight` after layout, then iteratively scale the verse text down (8% step, minK = 0.4 floor, 16-iter cap) until it fits without clipping. Wired at end-of-render via `requestAnimationFrame` (so layout is finalised before measure) and also fires on resize via 80 ms debounce. Covers all 6 verse-display surfaces via the v0.7.158 single-renderer architecture (Live Display, secondary screen, projector, in-app Settings preview, NDI broadcast, OBS Browser Source) without per-surface duplication. Static proof at `public/_lt-autofit-proof.html` was used for review and is removed in this ship.

**Files**:
- `src/lib/store.ts` (DisplayMode type narrow + persist migration v4→v5 + addDetectedVerse auto-nav fire — ~80 lines)
- `src/components/views/settings.tsx` (Display Mode picker delete + Lower Third Settings card delete — ~340 lines removed, replaced by ~12-line comment block)
- `src/components/layout/easyworship-shell.tsx` (Output Display Mode dropdown delete + modeMenuOpen state cleanup + status pill ternary collapse — ~65 lines removed)
- `src/components/presenter/stable-stage.tsx` (red ring removal at line 160)
- `src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` (auto-fit helpers + rAF + 80 ms debounced resize — ~95 lines)
- `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.182.
- `public/_lt-autofit-proof.html` deleted (review-only artefact).

Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)** — never re-introduce in-app Lower Third UI without coordinated removal of: (1) the v4→v5 migration coercion (otherwise the migration silently flips the operator's freshly-chosen LT mode back to 'full' on next boot), (2) the `DisplayMode = 'full'` type narrowing in `store.ts:46`. The current architecture is "in-app surfaces are Full only, NDI broadcast can be Full or Lower Third via `ndiDisplayMode`." Mixing the two muddles the operator's mental model — they explicitly asked for the split.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)** — when removing UI for a state field that has a persist migration, ALWAYS bump the persist version and add a coercion branch. Skipping the bump means existing installs with stale state keep rendering UI-less surfaces in the deleted mode. Verify by `rg -n "version: [0-9]" src/lib/store.ts` and confirming the new version > the previous one.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)** — `addDetectedVerse` auto-nav: any future detection-pipeline action MUST decide whether to fire `requestNavigatorRef` based on whether the detection is operator-confirmed (≥50% confidence, authoritative) vs speculative (<50%, candidate). Authoritative → fire (current addDetectedVerse). Speculative → DO NOT fire (current addDetectedVerseCandidate). Auto-firing on speculative detections thrashes the navigator on every speech tick and erodes operator trust in the auto-nav.

**GUARD-RAIL (D)** — when narrowing a union type to suppress dead branches, prefer single-literal narrow (`type X = 'full'` over `type X = 'full' | never`) — TS compiler treats both identically but the single-literal form is less likely to be re-widened by a copy-paste that thinks `'full' | never` is intentionally extensible. The narrowing is THE enforcement mechanism for "this state field has only one valid value" — if a future agent re-widens it, the migration coercion silently masks the regression and the operator-removed UI silently comes back to life through other surfaces.

**GUARD-RAIL (E)** — `stable-stage.tsx` `isLive` prop is now a no-op cosmetically but MUST stay in the component signature. Removing it from the props interface would break every caller in `presenter/`, `views/live-display.tsx`, and downstream consumers. The pattern (keep prop, drop the visual) is the safe refactor — operator-explicit "remove the red ring" doesn't mean "rip out the prop plumbing."


## v0.7.181 — OBS Browser Source URL: bake current NDI settings into URL + strip OBS mentions from Settings UI

Operator follow-up after v0.7.180 ship: "OBS is not showing what output NDI is firing OBS out still firing from main App. when i told you disconnect anything applied to it i need only NDI output to applied to OBS only." v0.7.178 added `?ndi=1&transparent=1` to the OBS Browser Source URL builder, which fixed the route's `IS_NDI` gating but did NOT fix this second class of bug — the OBS surface was first-painting at default state (Full mode, lh=md, sc=1.0) because no `lowerThird=1` / `position` / `lh` / `sc` URL params were baked in. SSE eventually caught up and updated the OBS surface, but OBS Browser Source aggressively caches the initial paint, so operators reported "OBS still firing from main App" because the first frame OBS captured used `s.displayMode` (the Live Display body mode) and `lh=md` defaults — which visually matches the main app feed when the operator is on Full mode, and looks like a stale/wrong LT layout when the operator switched NDI to Lower Third mode.

**Root cause**: three NDI surfaces emit URLs to `/api/output/congregation`. Two of them (`FrameCapture` in `electron/main.ts` ~line 2316, the actual NDI broadcast going out to vMix; and `NdiPreviewSurface` in `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` ~line 1158, the operator's in-panel preview) bake the operator's current LT params into the URL — `lowerThird=1`, `position=top|bottom`, `lh=sm|md|lg`, `sc=<0.5..2>`. The third (`buildObsUrl()` / `browserFallbackUrl` in `ndi-output-panel.tsx` ~line 86 + 102) was static — it only emitted `?ndi=1&transparent=1`. The v0.7.5.1 changelog literally documented this bug class: *"the captured BrowserWindow used to wait for the SSE state push before applying the operator's slider values, so vMix grabbed the FIRST few frames at the renderer's default state (md / 1.0×)."* That fix landed for FrameCapture but was never applied to `buildObsUrl`. v0.7.178 then half-fixed the divergence by adding `?ndi=1` (so the route's `USE_NDI_OVERRIDES` chain fires) but missed the URL-bake half.

**Fix — `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` (~lines 67-115)**: replaced the two hand-written URL constants with a `buildCongregationParams()` helper that bakes the same four LT params FrameCapture bakes, then uses it in both `buildObsUrl(host, port)` and `browserFallbackUrl`. The helper reads via `useAppStore.getState().settings` (lazy read) instead of capturing the existing hook locals because the URL builders sit ABOVE the `lowerThirdHeightSetting` hook declaration (line 245) and we didn't want to restructure the surrounding effect+QR+handleCopyUrl ordering. The component is already subscribed to all four fields elsewhere (`ndiDisplayMode` line 138, `lowerThirdPosition` line 169, `ndiLowerThirdScale` line 174, `lowerThirdHeight` line 245) so any operator change re-renders the component, re-runs the URL builders, re-reads `getState()` — fresh values every render with zero TDZ risk.

**Side effect intentionally accepted**: the OBS URL string changes when the operator changes NDI Display Mode / position / height bucket / scale — operator must re-copy + re-paste into OBS for first-paint accuracy after such tweaks. Operator confirmed this tradeoff is acceptable ("I rarely change those mid-service"). After paste, SSE drives all live updates (font, colour, line-height, slider drags, etc.) so no re-paste is needed for everyday tweaks.

**Strip 3 OBS mentions from Settings UI text** (operator-explicit follow-up: "i need only NDI section to have that control over OBS Browser Source URL"): (1) `src/components/views/settings.tsx` ~line 1062 — removed sentence "The OBS Browser Source URL is controlled exclusively from the NDI Output panel (v0.7.178)." from Lower Third Settings card description; (2) ~line 1493 — wireless display amber tip changed "with NDI Tools, AirPlay, Chromecast, OBS, vMix, or Wirecast" → "with NDI Tools, AirPlay, or Chromecast" (OBS / vMix / Wirecast are NDI-section concerns, not Settings concerns); (3) ~line 2581 — install AlertDialog changed "vMix / OBS / Wirecast will lose the source" → "NDI receivers will lose the source." Settings page is now silent about OBS; only the NDI Output panel mentions OBS.

**Files**: `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` (~50 lines: new `buildCongregationParams` helper + 2 URL builder rewrites + ~30 lines of inline comments documenting the 3-surface lockstep), `src/components/views/settings.tsx` (3 surgical text edits), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.181. Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: any new `/api/output/congregation` URL emitter targeting NDI broadcast or downstream NDI consumers (vMix / OBS Browser Source / Wirecast) MUST bake the four LT params (`lowerThird=1`, `position`, `lh`, `sc`) into the URL when `ndiDisplayMode === 'lower-third'`, in addition to `?ndi=1&transparent=1`. Skipping the bake means the receiver first-paints at Full mode + default scale until SSE catches up — and OBS Browser Source caches that first frame. Three emitters now in lockstep: `electron/main.ts` FrameCapture (~line 2316), `NdiPreviewSurface` (~line 1158), `buildObsUrl()` / `browserFallbackUrl` (~line 96). Adding a fourth (e.g. a future `<vmix-direct-url>` or `<wirecast-rtmp-url>` builder) MUST follow the same 4-param bake.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: when adding store reads inside a function declared ABOVE the matching hook subscription (TDZ scenario), use `useAppStore.getState().settings.<field>` (lazy read) instead of moving hooks around or restructuring effect ordering. This is safe ONLY when the component is already subscribed to those fields somewhere else in the render tree — otherwise React won't re-render on changes and the lazy read returns stale data forever. Verify by `rg -n "useAppStore\(\(s\) => s.settings.<field>\)" <file>` before adding a `getState()` read.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: do NOT remove "OBS / vMix / Wirecast" mentions from `ndi-output-panel.tsx` itself — those mentions are correct in the NDI panel (NDI feeds those receivers; that's the panel's whole job). The v0.7.181 strip is Settings-page-only. The mental model the operator wants: every OBS-related word lives in ONE place (NDI Output panel), so they always know where to go.

## v0.7.180 — File upload fix (Chromium 130+ trusted-UI hardening on hidden inputs)

Operator follow-up after v0.7.179 ship: postimg `V5NMM6kx` showed Custom Background "Click to upload background" dropzone in the installed Windows app. Operator's report: "Uploading anything from app is not working, not even the background." Diagnostic question (asked before any code change this time, per operator's explicit instruction "let me see before shipping") narrowed it down: **file picker opens, operator picks a file, then nothing visible happens — no spinner, no toast, no error**. That signature pinpoints the bug exactly: `fileInputRef.current?.click()` reaches the picker (so .click() works), but the input's `onChange` handler never fires after the operator picks a file → `handleUploadBackground` never runs → no `setIsUploading(true)` → no spinner → no fetch → no toast.

**Root cause**: Chromium 130+ shipped a "trusted UI" hardening that suppresses the `change` event on file inputs that are NOT in the layout tree (i.e. `display: none`) when the picker was triggered by a programmatic `.click()` relay (rather than a user-initiated activation through label-for or a visible click target). Both upload entry points in this codebase used the failing pattern: a visible div/button with `onClick={() => ref.current?.click()}` plus a sibling `<input type="file" className="hidden">` (Tailwind `.hidden` resolves to `display: none`). The hardening landed in stable Chromium ~130 (mid-2024) and Electron's bundled Chromium tracks stable Chromium with a few months lag — the regression bites packaged builds (latest Electron bundle) but NOT Turbopack dev (which uses the user's already-installed system browser, often a different channel/version), which is why the bug was invisible in dev.

**Fix — two surgical edits**:

1. **`src/components/views/settings.tsx` (~lines 1888-1934)** — Custom Background uploader: replaced the `<div onClick={…}>…</div>` + sibling `<input className="hidden">` pair with a single native `<label htmlFor="bg-upload-input">…<input id="bg-upload-input" className="sr-only">…</label>` pairing. The label-for relationship is one of HTML's oldest mechanisms — Chromium routes the click straight from the label to the input through the "labelable element" semantics it has supported since the spec was written, no JS .click() relay involved, so the trusted-UI hardening doesn't fire. The input class changes from `"hidden"` (display:none → out of layout tree) to `"sr-only"` (positioned absolutely off-screen, opacity:0, but still in the layout tree → satisfies trusted-UI). Visual rendering of the dropzone is byte-identical — only the wrapper element changed from `<div>` to `<label>`. The `fileInputRef` ref is retained on the input so `handleUploadBackground` can still reset `.value = ''` after upload to allow re-picking the same file.

2. **`src/components/layout/logos-shell.tsx` (~lines 2893-2921)** — Media Library uploader: minimum-risk fix — kept the existing `<input>` + Button onClick + dropzone onClick structure (because there are TWO trigger sites, restructuring to label would require duplicating the input or restructuring the Card layout) and just swapped `className="hidden"` → `className="sr-only"`. Same root-cause fix: the input is now in the layout tree, so the `.click()` relay from both `onClick={onPick}` triggers (Upload button at ~line 2928, empty-state dropzone at ~line 3026) is accepted as trusted and the change event fires.

**Files**: `src/components/views/settings.tsx` (~25 lines: div→label restructure + input id/class swap + comment block), `src/components/layout/logos-shell.tsx` (~12 lines: input class swap + comment block — input stays at top of Card, both triggers unchanged), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.180. Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean.

**Why we didn't ship a "guess" first**: previous round (v0.7.179) shipped the masterCode fix without showing the operator first, and they explicitly pushed back: "have you fix this; Uploading anything from app is not working, not even the background. you have the work ready from the begin? if you do listen them and let me see before shipping them." For v0.7.180 we asked one targeted diagnostic question (which exact failure mode of 4 plausible ones), got a precise answer pointing straight at the change-event drop, proposed the label-for fix in chat with code preview, and only shipped after explicit "Yes, ship v0.7.180" approval. Going forward, every operator-reported bug fix follows: diagnose → propose with code preview → wait for approval → ship.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: NEVER use `<input type="file" className="hidden">` paired with a programmatic `.click()` relay in this codebase. Use either: (a) a `<label htmlFor>` pairing wrapping a visible trigger + `className="sr-only"` on the input (preferred for single-trigger sites — the settings.tsx pattern), OR (b) `className="sr-only"` on the input + the existing `.click()` relay (acceptable for multi-trigger sites where label restructuring is invasive — the logos-shell.tsx pattern). Both keep the input in the layout tree. The `.hidden` class is fine for any element that is NOT a file input (text labels, decorative spans, etc.) — the trusted-UI hardening is specific to file inputs.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: when adding a NEW file upload entry point, also include a `console.error` inside the upload handler's catch block so any future packaged-build failure surfaces in DevTools (Ctrl+Shift+I → Console) for faster diagnosis. The current `toast.error(err.message)` is operator-facing but doesn't help when the handler never fires at all (current bug class). The console.error isn't strictly needed for THIS fix because the trusted-UI suppression itself is silent at the handler level — but for any future regression where the handler DOES fire and then fails, the console trail accelerates root-cause analysis.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: any future codebase-wide audit for this bug class should grep for `type="file"` AND `className="hidden"` on the same element (or within 5 lines) — `rg -nC 5 'type="file"' src/ | rg -B 3 -A 3 'className="hidden"'` finds every instance. At time of this fix the audit returns ZERO matches outside the two sites already fixed.

## v0.7.179 — Per-PC masterCode default = baked cloud admin code (cross-device sync auto-connects)

Operator follow-up after v0.7.178 ship: postimg `HL0xYmpX` showed the Admin Panel → Cross-Device Sync row with "wrong key — set up" badge in red, plus the per-PC Master Code field showing a random value (`SL-MASTER-KQM64N9F-D9QWEDXZ`) that did NOT match the cloud's masterCode. Operator's request: "change the master code to default for every installed app, same as the phone master code so the cloud can sync." (The "phone master code" = the canonical cloud admin code = `BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE` rotated to `SL-MASTER-HETEVT56-HCKTTS74` in v0.7.176.)

**Root cause**: `freshFile()` in `src/lib/licensing/storage.ts` (~line 412) generated a per-PC random masterCode via `generateMasterCode()` on every fresh install (`masterCode: envMasterCodeOverride() ?? generateMasterCode()`), and `load()` (~line 525) chained the same way — so the persisted `licenseFile.masterCode` was always a brand-new random value unique to that PC. Meanwhile the cross-device sync code path in `cloud-sync-test/route.ts` (line 67 fallback chain: per-PC `cfg.cloudAdminCode` → env `SCRIPTURELIVE_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE` → `BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE`) always sent the BAKED code to the cloud, but the cloud's `masterCode` (set via `SCRIPTURELIVE_MASTER_CODE` env var on the Replit deployment, also = baked) was being compared against THAT, and that part actually worked. The failing piece was a SEPARATE check: the Admin Panel header pill comparison between `licenseFile.masterCode` (random per-PC) and the cloud's masterCode → mismatch → "wrong key" badge. Operator's mental model: "the masterCode I see in the Admin Panel SHOULD be the masterCode my cloud uses — they're the same thing." We were artificially generating divergence.

**Fix — `src/lib/licensing/storage.ts` (3 surgical edits, ~50 lines added with comment blocks)**:
1. **Top-of-file import (~line 25)**: added `import { BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE } from '@/lib/baked-credentials'` so the storage layer can reach the canonical cloud admin code without going through the licensing chain.
2. **`freshFile()` masterCode default (~line 425)**: changed from `envMasterCodeOverride() ?? generateMasterCode()` → `envMasterCodeOverride() ?? (BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE || generateMasterCode())`. Every fresh install now persists the baked code as its per-PC masterCode → Admin Panel shows it identically to the cloud's. `generateMasterCode()` only fires if BOTH env override AND baked are empty (impossible in shipped builds — `baked-credentials.ts` always exports a non-empty literal).
3. **`load()` masterCode default (~line 545)**: same chain extension for the load path's "missing field on disk" branch (`envMasterCodeOverride() ?? parsed.masterCode ?? BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE ?? generateMasterCode()`).
4. **Self-heal block extension (~line 600)**: the existing v0.7.172 self-heal block re-pinned masterCode when `envMasterCodeOverride()` was set and disagreed with disk (cloud-deployment scenario). Added a parallel `else if (!override && BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE && parsed.masterCode && parsed.masterCode !== BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE)` branch: on every existing install (created before v0.7.179, persisted random masterCode on disk), the FIRST boot of v0.7.179 silently rewrites `cache.masterCode = BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE` and calls `persist(cache)`. Subsequent boots find them equal and skip — idempotent. No-op for cloud (env override branch wins) and no-op for fresh installs (freshFile already pinned).

**Side-effect intentionally accepted**: any operator who had memorised their old random masterCode as their LOCAL admin password no longer has that password work after this upgrade — they must use the baked code `SL-MASTER-HETEVT56-HCKTTS74` (or fall back to the per-PC `adminPassword` config field) to regain admin access. We accept this trade-off because (a) the new masterCode is shown verbatim in Admin Panel → Install + Master section so it's immediately discoverable, and (b) this is exactly what the operator explicitly requested ("same as the phone master code").

**Files**: `src/lib/licensing/storage.ts` (+~55 lines: 1 import + 3 expression edits + 1 self-heal else-if branch with full comment block), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.179. Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: `BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE` is now reached from THREE call sites in lockstep: `cloudPullAdminLedger()` chain (`cloud-sync.ts` resolver), `cloud-sync-test/route.ts` (header-pill / Sync Now button), and `licensing/storage.ts` (per-PC masterCode default). Rotating the constant in `baked-credentials.ts` MUST stay in lockstep with `inject-keys.mjs:173` AND every install on the previous baked permanently shows "wrong key" until they upgrade — see v0.7.176 GUARD-RAIL (D) for the upgrade-rotation playbook. Pre-v0.7.179, only the cloud chain consumed the baked value; from v0.7.179 forward the per-PC masterCode also tracks it.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: NEVER make `licensing/storage.ts` import another `licensing/*` file that itself imports `storage.ts` — the new `baked-credentials` import is safe because `baked-credentials.ts` is a leaf module (no upstream imports). Adding a new helper that reads from `licenseFile` AND is imported by storage would create a circular dependency that `tsc --noEmit` catches but webpack tolerates with `undefined` exports at runtime — silent boot failure on the licensing path.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: the self-heal branch is order-sensitive. The `else if (!override && ...)` MUST come AFTER the `if (override && ...)` env-override branch — flipping the order would let the baked rebake fire on cloud deployments and clobber the env override on every boot until the next persist round-trip. Verify with: env-set + persisted=BAKED → no persist call; env-set + persisted=random → persist (env wins); env-unset + persisted=random → persist (baked wins); env-unset + persisted=BAKED → no persist call (idempotent steady state).

## v0.7.178 — OBS Browser Source URL → NDI Settings (parity with NDI broadcast)

Operator follow-up after v0.7.177 ship: two annotated screenshots (postimg `CnC4zrNG` + `VdrfPyKv`) — the right-hand "NDI LIVE PREVIEW" card showed the verse rendered with NDI typography (Romans 8:23 ASV, full-screen full layout in screenshot 1, lower-third bar in screenshot 2), while the OBS Browser Source URL panel below ("SAME PC AS SCRIPTURELIVE AI" → `http://127.0.0.1:47330/api/output/congregation?transparent=…`, "OBS ON ANOTHER PC" → `http://192.168.11.184:47330/api/…`) was producing a DIFFERENT-looking feed — operator's red arrows pointed from the OBS URL fields straight at the NDI preview to make the requirement explicit: "Make all output of OBS Browser Source URL pick the same output settings from NDI Full display and Lower third display and disable it from receiving output from elsewhere; only NDI output source is applied to OBS Browser Source URL."

**Root cause**: `buildObsUrl()` and `browserFallbackUrl` in `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` (lines 68-69, 85) emitted `?transparent=1` ONLY — they never set `?ndi=1`. The `/api/output/congregation` route reads `?ndi=1` at line 276 (`IS_NDI=(__qp.get('ndi')==='1')`), and `IS_NDI` is the SOLE gate on `USE_NDI_OVERRIDES = IS_NDI` (line 762). Without it, the OBS feed fell into `USE_LT_OVERRIDES = !IS_NDI && dm.indexOf('lower-third')===0` (line 763) when the operator was in Lower Third mode — pulling the in-app `lowerThird*` typography pile (carved off in v0.7.167) — or into the body settings fallback (`fontSize`, `fontFamily`, `bibleLineHeight`, etc.) in Full mode. Result: every OBS scene rendered with completely different fonts / colours / line-height / aspect ratio / lower-third frame size from what the operator was watching in the NDI Live Preview — a silent drift that only became obvious once the operator put the two side-by-side.

**Fix — `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` (~lines 67-90, 102-107)**: both URL builders now emit `?ndi=1&transparent=1`. The `?transparent=1` keeps the v0.7.155 alpha-matte behaviour (clean cutout for OBS layering), and `?ndi=1` flips `IS_NDI=true` server-side, which propagates through the entire downstream resolver chain by construction:

- **Display mode**: `(IS_NDI && st.ndiDisplayMode) ? st.ndiDisplayMode : (s.displayMode||'full')` (route.ts ~line 727) — toggling NDI Full / Lower Third in the panel above the URL card now flips OBS instantly without touching the projector display mode.
- **Typography pile**: every `T_*` resolver (`T_FF`/`T_FS`/`T_SH_BOOL`/`T_TA`/`T_SCALE`/`T_COLOR`/`T_LH`) starts with `(USE_NDI_OVERRIDES && st.ndi*) ? st.ndi* : ...` so the `ndiFontFamily`/`ndiFontSize`/`ndiTextShadow`/`ndiTextAlign`/`ndiTextScale`/`ndiBibleColor`/`ndiBibleLineHeight` pile (operator-tuneable from this same NDI Output panel) wins on every slot.
- **Reference typography**: `ndiRefSize`/`ndiRefStyle`/`ndiRefPosition`/`ndiRefScale` chain inherits via `IS_NDI` checks at the route's reference resolver (~line 850).
- **Lower-third frame**: `ndiLowerThirdScale` / `ndiLowerThirdHeight` / `ndiLowerThirdPosition` / `ndiLowerThirdTransparent` all gated on `USE_NDI_OVERRIDES` — operator's NDI bucket buttons + transparent-LT toggle now drive OBS too.
- **Aspect ratio**: `(IS_NDI && st.ndiAspectRatio && st.ndiAspectRatio!=='auto') ? st.ndiAspectRatio : (st.displayRatio||'fill')` (route.ts ~line 770) — NDI aspect override wins.
- **Translation**: `ndiTranslation` overrides `selectedTranslation` for OBS feed.
- **Background**: `ndiCustomBackground` / `ndiTheme` overrides body equivalents.

**The "disable receiving from elsewhere" requirement**: built into the route by construction — `USE_NDI_OVERRIDES` and `USE_LT_OVERRIDES` are mutually exclusive on `IS_NDI` (only one branch evaluates true at a time), so once `?ndi=1` is in the URL the in-app `lowerThird*` settings literally cannot reach the OBS surface. The body/projector settings only contribute when an `ndi*` override is undefined (intentional fallback so a partial NDI override profile still works), which matches the operator's mental model: "fill in the NDI fields you care about, leave the rest blank to mirror Live."

**Files**: `src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` (~24 lines: 2 URL builder edits + ~20 lines of explanatory comments), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.178.

**Tests**: `tsc --noEmit` clean. Live curl proof: `curl -s "http://localhost:80/api/output/congregation?transparent=1"` returns the same HTML bundle as `?ndi=1&transparent=1` (route is parameter-driven), but the runtime `IS_NDI=(__qp.get('ndi')==='1')` (route.ts line 276) only resolves true on the new URL — so the entire `USE_NDI_OVERRIDES` resolver chain only fires for the v0.7.178 URL string.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: any new `/api/output/congregation` URL emitter that targets the NDI broadcast or any OBS/vMix downstream surface MUST include `?ndi=1`. Without it the surface silently inherits the in-app `lowerThird*` pile (LT mode) or body settings (Full mode) and operators experience the v0.7.178 root cause again. Existing `?ndi=1` emitters at time of writing: `electron/main.ts` FrameCapture URL chain (NDI broadcast capture), `NdiPreviewSurface` ((operator's in-panel preview, byte-identical to OBS feed), `buildObsUrl()` / `browserFallbackUrl` (this fix). Any new broadcast surface must follow the same pattern.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: NEVER make `IS_NDI` default to true on the route. The Live Display window, secondary screen, projector, in-app Settings preview, and Bible Lookup preview all rely on `IS_NDI=false` so the body / `lowerThird*` settings reach them. Flipping the default would silently break every non-broadcast surface in lockstep.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: when adding a NEW `ndi*` setting (a new override field), always wire its T_* resolver as `(USE_NDI_OVERRIDES && st.ndiNewField !== undefined) ? st.ndiNewField : (USE_LT_OVERRIDES && st.lowerThirdNewField !== undefined) ? st.lowerThirdNewField : st.bodyEquivalent` so the 3-tier chain stays consistent. Skipping the LT tier means the in-app LT preview will silently track the body setting instead of the LT setting (the v0.7.167 mistake we already paid for). Skipping the NDI tier means OBS will silently track LT/body instead of NDI (the v0.7.178 mistake we are now fixing).

## v0.7.177 — In-App LT Preview WYSIWYG Geometry (native 1920×1080 transform-scale)

Operator follow-up after v0.7.176 ship: "the lower third settings are still wrong; from the live preview box text is not well fit in the lower third main frame even with Bible line-height (0.90) and Bible text scale (0.50) dragged to the end. The frame is too small; you need to set it like the way the NDI lower third is shown — neat frame, text fits."

**Root cause**: `OutputPreview` (`src/components/settings/output-preview.tsx`, used by every Settings card preview AND the in-app Live Display) sized its iframe to fit the parent card directly (`w-full h-full`), so the iframe's INNER VIEWPORT equalled the card's physical pixel size — typically ~360-460 px wide in a settings card. At that size the LT box at hPct=22 % is only ~50-60 px tall, and the `clamp(.5rem, ..., 2rem)` font-size formula floors at the minimum, so 4-line verses (Romans 8:23 ASV ≈ 200 chars) crashed into the rounded edges with no breathing room. The actual NDI broadcast (FrameCapture window in Electron main) and `NdiPreviewSurface` (NDI Output panel preview, v0.7.9) already used the correct technique: render the iframe at NATIVE 1920×1080 then CSS `transform: scale()` into the card. That gives the iframe a 1920 px-wide internal viewport → LT box = 237 px tall → ltBand 9vw = 173 px font (auto-clamped to ltCap 3.2rem) → text fits exactly like the operator's KKX4HcF8 reference (Romans 8:23 chyron over portrait video).

**Fix — `src/components/settings/output-preview.tsx` (+~40 lines, JSX wrapper restructured)**: added `wrapperRef` + `NATIVE_W`/`NATIVE_H` constants (1920×1080 default, 1440×1080 for 4:3, 2520×1080 for 21:9, all height-pinned) + `ResizeObserver`-driven `scale` state + `useLayoutEffect` measurement; iframe now lives inside an inner `<div>` of fixed `width:NATIVE_W; height:NATIVE_H` with `transform: scale(${scale})` and `transformOrigin: 'top left'`. Outer wrapper keeps the same `aspect-ratio` + `overflow:hidden` so the rendered card is visually identical — only the internal pixel coordinates change. Ported byte-for-byte from `NdiPreviewSurface` (`src/components/views/ndi-output-panel.tsx` ~line 1100, lines 1185-1219). All consumers automatically inherit the fix (no API change): every Settings → Lower Third card, the Display tab Full Screen + Lower Third side-by-side previews, the in-app Live Display pane (`logos-shell.tsx`), the Bible Lookup preview, the Main Preview pane.

**NDI broadcast surface untouched** — the `NdiPreviewSurface` always rendered correctly; the FrameCapture URL chain (`?ndi=1&transparent=1&lh=…&sc=…`) was never the bug. Likewise `route.ts` itself is unmodified — same hPct=22 lock, same dark-gradient default, same opt-in `.lt-box.transparent` for NDI alpha keying — so v0.7.176 GR-A (frame-lock) and GR-B (dark default) are preserved exactly.

**Fix B — LT-only Bible line-height floor + default raised (`src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` ~line 827)**: Operator follow-up after Fix A landed: "the after text all cramble no space inbetween text — adjust only the text not the frame is ok." Even with the geometry corrected, lines were touching because (a) the operator had previously dragged `lowerThirdBibleLineHeight` to the 0.9 minimum trying to make text fit when it was overflowing, and (b) the unset default fell through to the body `bibleLineHeight` chain → 0 (no override) → CSS rule `.lt-box .slide-text{line-height:1.25}` from line 167. Carved the LT branch off the body fallback chain: when `USE_LT_OVERRIDES` is true, the operator's `lowerThirdBibleLineHeight` is now clamped to `Math.max(1.2, ...)` (was 0.9 floor), and the unset default is `1.4` (was 0 = inherit body). NDI branch (`USE_NDI_OVERRIDES && st.ndiBibleLineHeight`) is byte-identical — the broadcast feed still allows the 0.9 tight floor for vMix/OBS-NDI operators whose own framing demands it. Body fallback (line 840-842) is byte-identical. Frame `hPct=22` is UNCHANGED — operator-explicit ("not the frame").

**Files**: `src/components/settings/output-preview.tsx` (+~40 lines, Fix A), `src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` (Fix B: ~10 lines, LT-only T_LH branch), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.177.

**Tests**: `tsc --noEmit` clean. Side-by-side proof page (`public/_lt-proof.html`, deleted after capture) demonstrated the BEFORE chyron text overflowing the bottom of a 460-px card vs AFTER fitting the full Romans 8:23 ASV in 2 lines with breathing room — saved as `screenshots/lt-preview-proof-v0.7.177.jpg`.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: any new in-app preview surface that loads `/api/output/congregation` MUST render at NATIVE_W × NATIVE_H + transform-scale, NOT iframe-fits-parent. Sizing the iframe to physical card pixels is the root cause class — `cqw`/`cqh`/`vw` font math floors at the clamp minimum on small viewports and verses crash the box. Use `OutputPreview` (now correct) or copy the `NdiPreviewSurface` / new `OutputPreview` wrapper pattern.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: NATIVE_H is pinned at 1080; NATIVE_W flexes by aspect (16:9 → 1920, 4:3 → 1440, 21:9 → 2520). Adding a new `displayRatio` MUST add the matching NATIVE_W branch — anything else falls back to 1920 and the chyron will be the wrong width relative to the card.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: do NOT delete the v0.7.9 NdiPreviewSurface scale logic or merge it into `OutputPreview` — they are intentional duplicates. NdiPreviewSurface owns the WYSIWYG of the actual NDI broadcast (with `?ndi=1` + freeze-after-mount semantics), `OutputPreview` owns the WYSIWYG of every other surface (postMessage data plane). Merging would couple the NDI capture's frozen-src lifecycle to every settings preview and reintroduce the v0.7.11 "blank-then-paint flash" on every slider drag.

## v0.7.176 — Lower-Third Frame Lock + Text Auto-Fit Boost + Status Badge Removal + Cloud MasterCode Rotation

Operator follow-up after v0.7.175: (a) "the lower-third on the in-app preview looks neat but what's going out to the Live Display window, secondary screen and OBS Browser Source URL keeps changing size when I click Small/Medium/Large in Settings — the bar must NOT change size between surfaces, frame must remain Small bucket for ALL", (b) "take this off from the app" (annotated screenshot of bottom status bar pointing at the green "NDI / Output Active" badge), (c) "the admin clouding still want me to type Master Code before it sync, because the master codes are different from each other — could you make both master code the same? SL-MASTER-HETEVT56-HCKTTS74".

**Fix A — Lower-third plate restored to dark gradient default (`src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` ~line 104)**: reverted the v0.7.173 Fix B transparent-default. The default `.lt-box` is back to the dark rounded card with `box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.45)` and `background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0a0a0a,#171717)`, and all 6 `.lt-box.theme-*` themed gradients (sermon brown, worship blue, etc.) are restored. The opt-IN `.lt-box.transparent` class (set when the operator flips `ndiLowerThirdTransparent` on for vMix/OBS alpha-key receivers) still drops the gradient + shadow — so NDI broadcast keying still works exactly as designed in v0.7.173, but the in-room projector / Live Display / OBS browser source default is back to a readable broadcast-style chyron card. The v0.7.173 rationale ("transparent so it doesn't bleed into video feeds") was correct for true NDI capture but wrong as the global default — operators on a live stage want the dark card by default and opt OUT for NDI alpha keys, not the other way round.

**Fix B — LT frame frozen at SMALL (22%) on every surface, every bucket (`src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` ~line 1053)**: locked `__lhKey='sm'` and `hPct=22` UNCONDITIONALLY — no per-surface conditional, no `IS_NDI` branch, no `lowerThirdHeight` lookup. Operator's Small/Medium/Large bucket button in Settings → Lower Third no longer changes the FRAME size on ANY surface served by this route (in-app LT preview, Live Display window, secondary screen, OBS browser source URL, NDI feed). The buttons remain functional for typography (font size, alignment, theme still flow through the regular `st.fontSize`/`st.textAlign`/`st.congregationScreenTheme` chain) — only the frame height is now operator-bucket-immune. Rationale: operators have ALREADY framed their broadcast camera slot before the service starts, and the bar size has to stay where they put it; if Small/Medium/Large jumped the frame size mid-service the broadcast comp would have to be re-framed every time. Earlier in this same session a draft "respect operator bucket" build was tested and rejected by the operator on the spot ("Noooo, frame size mush remain the same for all, it can not change for Medium bucket, Large bucket, it must remain Small bucket for all whitout changing in sizes") — so the lock is the explicitly-requested final behaviour, not a fallback.

**Fix C — Text auto-fit boosted so the verse fills the locked Small frame (`src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` ~line 1099)**: `ltBand` ramp bumped from `5/7/9/11` → `7/9/12/15` vw and `ltCap` from `2.0` → `3.2` rem. With the frame now locked at sm (22% of frame ≈ 158px on 720p), the previous text sizing was undersized — short verses rendered ~28px and looked lost in a comparatively large card. The new ramp makes a ~140-char verse render around the same visual weight as the in-app Settings preview reference (operator's sX6zjqV target), with the existing `clamp(min, container-query, max)` math doing the per-length shrink so 320+ char verses still fit on 2 lines without overflow. The long-verse end of the ramp (320+ chars → 7vw) is intentionally less aggressive than the short-verse end (≤90 chars → 15vw) because the auto-fit headroom shrinks as text density rises.

**Fix D — `.lower-third` `align-items:stretch` retained from v0.7.173**: the parent flex container keeps `align-items:stretch` so Chromium does not collapse the child `.lt-box` `height:100%` to intrinsic on any layout pass — the locked 22% bucket survives every aspect-ratio change (tall preview iframes, wide secondary screens, NDI 16:9 captures). This was the v0.7.173 Fix D regression-fix and is preserved verbatim; without it, Fix B above would still visibly shrink the frame to hug short text on certain surfaces.

**Fix E — Lower-third side padding tightened to `0 17.5%`**: the chyron card now renders centred at ~65% width matching the operator's reference screenshot (pvktjHxy), instead of spanning full-width minus tiny 2.5% gutters which made it look like a HUD bar instead of a broadcast caption. The `lt-content` text band still uses `text-align:center` per the operator's `textAlign:'center'` default, so the verse stays centred inside the narrower card.

**Fix F — "NDI / Output Active" status badge removed (`src/components/layout/easyworship-shell.tsx` ~line 1541)**: per operator's annotated screenshot (postimg.cc/F1vTvPYC) marking the bottom-right of the TransportBar. Removed the `<Radio>` icon + `<span>` label "NDI / Output Active" / "Output Idle" + the preceding vertical divider. The Radio import is RETAINED (still used elsewhere in the file at line 284 for an unrelated indicator) and the `outputActive` state IS RETAINED (still drives the actual NDI activation chain at lines 1565+, the `TopToolbar outputActive` lamp, the auto-enable side-effect when `outputDestination === 'ndi'`, and the keyboard-shortcut toggle handler) — only the visual chip in the bottom status strip is gone. Net effect: cleaner status bar, zero behavioural change to NDI / output broadcasting.

**Fix G — Cloud admin masterCode rotated back to `SL-MASTER-HETEVT56-HCKTTS74`**: the v0.7.173 rotation to `SL-MASTER-FSFWDV75-WHBMXF4G` mismatched the operator's actual cloud `SCRIPTURELIVE_MASTER_CODE` env var, so every fresh v0.7.173+ install was prompted for a code in Settings → Cross-Device Admin Sync. Reverted the baked constant to the original v0.7.161 value (`SL-MASTER-HETEVT56-HCKTTS74`) which matches what the operator now has set on the cloud deploy. Lockstep updated in 3 places per the v0.7.173 GUARD-RAIL D: `src/lib/baked-credentials.ts:26` (the gitignored constant), `scripts/inject-keys.mjs:173` (the CI hardcoded fallback), and `src/lib/licensing/storage.ts:376, 389` (the comment references annotating the precedence chain). Every fresh v0.7.176+ install now auto-pairs with cloud out-of-the-box. CAVEAT: existing PCs running v0.7.160–v0.7.175 that EVER had a per-PC `cloudAdminCode` saved in their license file (typed in via Settings → Cross-Device Admin Sync at any point) will still need to either clear that field or overwrite it with the new code, because per-PC override wins over baked per the v0.7.161 precedence chain (intentional — lets operators repoint a single PC at a different cloud install).

**Files**: `src/app/api/output/congregation/route.ts` (5 surgical edits — plate default + theme gradients + frame lock + padding + auto-fit ramp), `src/components/layout/easyworship-shell.tsx` (badge block removed), `src/lib/baked-credentials.ts` (constant rotated), `scripts/inject-keys.mjs` (fallback rotated), `src/lib/licensing/storage.ts` (comment refs refreshed), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.176, dev-only `public/_lt-proof.html` deleted. Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean.

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: the lower-third frame height (`hPct`) MUST stay locked at the Small bucket (22%) and MUST NOT key off `st.lowerThirdHeight` on any surface served by `route.ts`. Any future PR that re-introduces an `hMap[__lhKey]` lookup or a per-bucket conditional needs to be rejected unless the operator explicitly asks for a per-surface different size — the recurring failure mode is "frame jumps on Live Display when operator clicks a bucket", which mid-service breaks the broadcast camera framing and is unrecoverable without re-framing the camera. The bucket buttons remain wired to typography (font size, scale, alignment, theme) which is the right place for operator-driven size variance.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: the LT plate default is the DARK gradient card (the v0.7.176 revert of v0.7.173 Fix B). NDI alpha-key receivers opt OUT via `ndiLowerThirdTransparent`, not the other way round. Any future "the bar bleeds into the video feed" complaint must be diagnosed FIRST as "is the operator on an NDI surface where ndiLowerThirdTransparent is OFF?" — flipping the global default back to transparent will silently break the Live Display / projector / OBS browser source surfaces that operators expect to render a readable card.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: the LT text auto-fit ramp (`ltBand` 7/9/12/15 vw, `ltCap` 3.2rem, `ltMin` 0.8rem) is tuned for the LOCKED 22% Small frame. Any future change to `hPct` (whether re-introducing the bucket lookup or rotating the locked value to md/lg) MUST re-tune this ramp in lockstep — bigger frames need a bigger `ltBand` ceiling so short verses don't look orphaned, smaller frames need a smaller `ltCap` so long verses don't overflow. Test fixture: 3 verses (≤90 / ~180 / ~320 chars) on 1280×720 viewport — none should overflow the rounded card, and the shortest should visually match the operator's Settings → Lower Third preview at 1× scale.

**GUARD-RAIL (D)**: rotating `BAKED_CLOUD_ADMIN_CODE` is destructive (carried over from v0.7.173 GR D). Every install on the previous baked value PERMANENTLY shows "wrong key" until they update. Rotate ONLY when the cloud's `SCRIPTURELIVE_MASTER_CODE` was generated independently and cannot be brought into sync. ALWAYS update BOTH `baked-credentials.ts:26` AND `inject-keys.mjs:173` AND refresh comment references in `storage.ts` (currently lines 376 + 389). NEW corollary: if an operator reports "still asking for master code after update", check FIRST whether they have a per-PC `cloudAdminCode` saved (overrides baked per v0.7.161 chain) — the rotation only fixes auto-pairing on FRESH installs and on existing installs that never typed a code into Settings.

**GUARD-RAIL (E)**: when removing a status indicator from the TransportBar / TopToolbar / any UI chip, ALWAYS keep the underlying state variable (`outputActive`, `isLive`, `ndiStatus`, etc.) and its dependent side-effect/auto-enable hooks intact. The removed chip is purely cosmetic; the state drives the actual hardware/broadcast behaviour. Verify with `rg -n "<varname>" <same-file>` after the edit — every other usage MUST still typecheck clean and behave identically.

## v0.7.175 — Hotfix: Untrack Installer Artwork from `.gitignore` (CI Windows Build Red on v0.7.173)

Operator: "v0.7.173 faild" — terse, but the v0.7.173 GitHub Actions release run came back red on the `Build & package Windows installer` step. Pulled the failed-step log from the Actions API and the root cause was electron-builder NSIS phase erroring with `cannot find specified resource "build-resources/installerHeader.bmp"` and `installerSidebar.bmp` (Errors #1 and #2, both fatal — `Cannot cleanup` followed by `ERR_PNPM_RECURSIVE_RUN_FIRST_FAIL` on `package:win`).

The files exist on the local Replit dev box (and have for months — last touched 2026-05-09 / 2026-05-10) but were NEVER tracked in git: `artifacts/imported-app/.gitignore` line 91 was a blanket `build-resources/*` rule with only 4 carve-outs (`notarize.js`, `icon.ico`, `icon.png`, `entitlements.mac.plist`). Every other installer asset — the two NSIS BMPs, the uninstaller BMP, `installer.nsh` (NSIS hook with `customInstall` MessageBox, see v0.7.142–143 GR), `license.txt` (the ASCII license per v0.7.139 GR), and the bundled NDI runtime DLL `build-resources/ndi/Processing.NDI.Lib.x64.dll` (28 MB, the v0.7.146 "no separate NDI Tools install required" guarantee) — was silently excluded from every git push.

CI checks out from git, finds an empty `build-resources/` (only 4 files), and electron-builder fails the moment NSIS goes to render the wizard chrome. The local Windows BUILD.bat path always worked because it operates against the live working tree, so this never surfaced before — the v0.7.173 Actions run was the first one where the missing assets were the only failure mode (prior CI reds were the v0.7.171 webpack `node:` scheme + v0.7.170 edge-runtime warnings, both now fixed, so the NSIS phase finally got reached and immediately exposed the missing artwork).

**Fix — `.gitignore` carve-outs (`artifacts/imported-app/.gitignore` lines 99-109)**: added 6 explicit `!build-resources/<asset>` lines covering `installerHeader.bmp`, `installerSidebar.bmp`, `uninstallerSidebar.bmp`, `installer.nsh`, `license.txt`, and the NDI directory (`!build-resources/ndi/` + `!build-resources/ndi/**`). The blanket `build-resources/*` rule is INTENTIONALLY kept on line 94 — the directory accumulates electron-builder cache junk (release/, win-unpacked/, etc.) that we never want in the repo, so the carve-out pattern is correct: deny by default + allow-list the exact runtime/branding assets. Pushed all 6 newly-tracked files (28 MB NDI DLL + ~350 KB BMPs + scripts) as part of the v0.7.175 commit.

**Files**: `artifacts/imported-app/.gitignore` (+11 lines), 6 newly-tracked binary/text assets in `build-resources/` (DLL + BMPs + NSH + license), `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.175. Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean (gitignore changes are CI-only, no source impact).

**GUARD-RAIL (A)**: any new file added under `artifacts/imported-app/build-resources/` that ships INTO the installer (referenced from `electron-builder.yml`, `installer.nsh`, or `extraResources`) MUST get a matching `!build-resources/<path>` line in `.gitignore` — the blanket `build-resources/*` denial WILL silently strip it from git and the failure won't surface until a CI run hits the NSIS/extraResources phase (which can be many commits downstream of the file's introduction). Verify after every add with `git --no-optional-locks check-ignore -v artifacts/imported-app/build-resources/<file>` — if it prints anything, you need a `!` carve-out.

**GUARD-RAIL (B)**: when triaging a CI red on the GitHub Actions Windows build, ALWAYS pull the failed step's log via the Actions REST API (`GET /repos/<owner>/<repo>/actions/runs/<id>/logs` returns a ZIP; the per-step files live under `Build Windows installer/<N>_<step-name>.txt`). The local Replit BUILD.bat operates on the working tree, not the git index, so any "file in working tree but not in git" failure is INVISIBLE locally and ONLY surfaces in CI — `pnpm typecheck` will never catch it.

**GUARD-RAIL (C)**: NEVER convert the `build-resources/*` rule into a flat `!build-resources/` allow-all. The directory is a working scratchpad for electron-builder (release/, win-unpacked/, win-arm64-unpacked/, .cache/, *.tmp) and accidentally tracking those bloats the repo by hundreds of MB per release cycle. Deny-by-default + explicit allow-list is the only correct pattern.

## v0.7.174 — Bible-Book Homophone Normaliser (Ruth → "Root"/"Roach" Field Bug)

Operator field-report after upgrading to v0.7.173: "i was saying Ruth chapter 6 verse 1 but see what came 'Roach top touch is best. Root. Root chapter 6 verses. Root chapter 6 verses.'"

Even though `Ruth` is in the `BIBLE_KEYTERMS` list fed to Deepgram Nova-3 (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/deepgram-keyterms.ts`) AND the renderer's direct WSS path (`use-deepgram-streaming.ts:420`) on every connection, Deepgram's keyterm boost is a SOFT bias — short single-syllable Bible book names ("Ruth", "Job", "Jude", "Acts") still come back as their nearest acoustic neighbours under noisy stage mics, Ghanaian English vowel shifts, or fast pulpit cadence. The streaming model isn't going to be re-trained from our side; the right place to fix this is one layer DOWNSTREAM in the verse parser, since EVERY mishear of a book name in a verse-reference context follows the same structural pattern: the wrong token sits immediately before "chapter", a digit, or a spoken number-word — a context window that is unique to verse references in English.

### Fix — `BOOK_HOMOPHONES` table + `normalizeBookHomophones()` (`src/lib/bible-api.ts` ~line 545)

10-row table of `[regex-pattern, correct-book-name]` pairs covering the highest-confidence mishears:

- Ruth ← root / roots / roach / roof / route / rooth / ruse
- Job ← joe / jobe
- Jude ← dude / jewed
- Amos ← ammos
- Titus ← tightest
- Habakkuk ← "had a cook" / "have a cook" (phrase-level)
- Malachi ← malarkey
- Joel ← jewel
- Acts ← axe / ax
- Hosea ← hoseah / hosier

The rewrite ONLY fires when the mishear is followed (allowing punctuation) by `chapter`, a 1-3 digit number, or a number-word (`one`/`two`/.../`hundred`/ordinals). That guard means everyday English ("the root of the problem", "Mark the date", "axe the budget") is NEVER touched — the regex looks like:

```
\b(root|roots|roach|...)\b[\s,.:;!?]+(chapter\b|\d{1,3}\b|<num-word-stem>\b)
```

per pattern. Hooked into the existing `normalizeSpokenNumbers()` as the FIRST step (before the ordinal-prefix pass) so the downstream number-word collapser ("six" → "6") and every regex pattern (1)-(7) in `BOOK_NAMES_PATTERN` see the corrected book name. Both call sites (`bible-api.ts:632` extract pipeline, `bible-api.ts:1226` parser entry) automatically inherit the fix.

### Files

- `artifacts/imported-app/src/lib/bible-api.ts` (+~52 lines: BOOK_HOMOPHONES table + NUM_WORD_STEM constant + normalizeBookHomophones() + 1-line wire into normalizeSpokenNumbers)
- `package.json` + `BUILD.bat` banner bumped to 0.7.174

Tests: `tsc --noEmit` clean.

### GUARD-RAILS

- **(A)** Every BOOK_HOMOPHONES entry MUST require a verse-reference context follower (`chapter`, digit, or number-word) — NEVER add a bare `\b<word>\b → <book>` rule. The whole reason this fix is safe is the structural uniqueness of "<word> chapter <num>" / "<word> <num> <num>" in English. A bare rewrite would clobber sermon prose ("the root of all evil", "axe to grind", "tightest grip").
- **(B)** When the operator reports a NEW mishear, add the pair to `BOOK_HOMOPHONES` — do NOT try to escalate Deepgram biasing (raising keyterm count past ~50 starts hurting recall on adjacent words; we're already at 80+). The downstream rewrite is cheaper, deterministic, and survives provider swaps (if we ever route some users through Whisper or browser STT, the homophone table still works).
- **(C)** Deepgram's keyterm boost is necessary but NOT sufficient for short single-syllable Bible book names. The acoustic neighbour set (Ruth↔root↔roach, Jude↔dude, Amos↔famous, Job↔joe) is a known limitation of Nova-3's English acoustic model on noisy live stages. Future single-syllable additions to the canon (none expected, but e.g. if we add Greek/Hebrew names) MUST get a homophone audit before the first field deployment.
